## Question 62

**Topic:** Logical Reasoning and Analytical Ability

### Question

Read the given statement and conclusions carefully. Assuming that the information given in the statements is true, even if it appears to be at variance with commonly known facts, decide which of the given conclusions logically follow(s) from the statement.

**Statement:** “I don’t want to pursue online marriage matchmaking because I fear the platform is unsafe and can invite fraud.” - A told B.

**Conclusions:**
(i) A believes in self-respect and expects the same from his spouse.
(ii) A wants to pursue marriage.

### Options

1. Only conclusion (i) follows
2. Only conclusion (ii) follows
3. Both the conclusions follow
4. Neither conclusion (i) nor (ii) follows

### Answer

> **Answer: 2**

Endowments-com-2021

## Question 74

**Topic:** Logical Reasoning and Analytical Ability

### Question

The following item consist of two Statements: one labelled as the ‘Assertion (A)’ and the other as ‘Reason (R)’. You are to examine these two statements carefully and select the answers to these items using the codes given below:

**Assertion (A):** Spirogyra, Ulothrix, Cladophora and Chara are grouped under Thallophyta.

**Reason (R):** Spirogyra, Ulothrix, Cladophora and Chara lack roots, stem, leaves, and vascular system.

### Options

1. Both A and R are individually true and R is the correct explanation of A
2. Both A and R are individually true but R is NOT the correct explanation of A
